> `911f5f7cdaa3fb53b9b5a2bdefb82e793c65eafb` (both ref files read with the file tools). It records

> 2026-09-12 on the user's ruling adding rows 65, 66 and 67; *former wordings, preserved #12: "(37 rows)"
> after row 22 was ruled in earlier that day, and "(36 rows)" before that*) and is not
> restated here; the order worked is that file's group order, "stated, not ruled".**
>
> **Why a separate file rather than a block appended to `candidacy_upgrades.md`, declared:** the L1
> slice's session appended its progress to that file. Appending through the bridge means rewriting the
> whole 32,054-byte file, and re-typing a file's content from tool output is the transcription risk the
> record forbids (a copy assembled by a shell over a staged copy would be a shell read of repository
> content, D-253). This file is the progress record for L2's slice and is pointed at from the handoff
> entry; whether it is later folded into `candidacy_upgrades.md` is a landing dispatch's matter.
>
> **★ THIS FILE WAS SPLIT ON 2026-09-07, ON THE USER'S RATIFICATION, AND NOTHING WAS DELETED.** It had

> here is the status surface a session needs at boot: the gate, the table, the counts, the carried

> file.**
>
> **The row numbers here are `candidacy_upgrades.md`'s.** `reading_pass/object_reads/row<n>_…` files
> are numbered by `reading_pass/population.md`'s table, a different numbering; the two must not be
> read across.
